User: Do you have any suggestions for a spicier version of this recipe, maybe with some added jalapeños or hot sauce?

Assistant:
Yes, here's a variation that will give your chicken enchiladas some extra heat:

Ingredients:

- 6-8 medium-sized tortillas
- 2 cups of cooked and shredded chicken
- 1 onion, chopped
- 1 green bell pepper, chopped
- 1 red bell pepper, chopped
- 2 garlic cloves, minced
- 1-2 jalapeño peppers, seeded and finely chopped
- 1 tbsp vegetable oil
- 2 tbsp tomato paste
- 2-3 tbsp hot sauce (depending on your desired heat level)
- 1 tsp chili powder
- 1/2 tsp ground cumin
- 1/2 tsp salt
- 1/4 tsp black pepper
- 2 cups enchilada sauce
- 1 1/2 cups grated cheddar cheese
- Fresh cilantro, chopped (optional)
- Sour cream (optional)

Instructions:

1. Preheat the oven to 375°F. 2. In a large skillet, heat the vegetable oil over medium-high heat. Add the onions, green and red bell peppers, jalapeños, and garlic; cook for 3-4 minutes, until the vegetables are soft. 3. Add the tomato paste, hot sauce, chili powder, ground cumin, salt, and black pepper to the pan. Cook, stirring frequently, for another 1-2 minutes. 4. Stir in the cooked chicken, and half of the enchilada sauce. Cook for a few minutes, stirring occasionally, until the chicken is heated through. 5. Spread some of the remaining enchilada sauce in the bottom of a 9x13 inch baking dish. 6. Take a tortilla and spoon some of the chicken mixture down the center of the tortilla. Roll up the tortilla and place it seam-side down in the baking dish. Repeat with the remaining tortillas and chicken mixture. 7. Pour the rest of the enchilada sauce over the top of the tortillas. 8. Sprinkle the grated cheese over the top of the enchiladas. 9. Bake in the preheated oven for 20-25 minutes, until the cheese is melted and bubbly. 10. Garnish with fresh cilantro and a dollop of sour cream (optional). Note: If you don't have hot sauce, you can substitute with a few dashes of cayenne pepper or red pepper flakes. And if you like your enchiladas even spicier, you can increase the amount of jalapeños or hot sauce to your liking.

User: Can you please add some information about how social media use affects academic performance in adolescents? I think it's an important factor to consider in the overall impact of social media on teens.

Assistant:
Absolutely! Below is the revised literature review section, including information on how social media use affects academic performance in adolescents. Literature Review

Self-esteem

Self-esteem is an essential part of adolescent development, and social media can impact it positively or negatively. Some studies have found that social media use can enhance self-esteem by allowing adolescents to project a positive self-image to others (Manago, Graham, Greenfield, & Salimbene, 2011). Social media use can also provide a platform for teens to express themselves creatively and showcase their talents, leading to increased self-esteem (Ratto, 2018). Body Image

Body image disturbances are common in adolescents, and social media use can exacerbate this problem. However, social media use can also have positive effects on body image. Research has shown that adolescents who follow body-positive influencers on social media have higher levels of body appreciation and self-acceptance (Spuhler, Wagner, & Gnambs, 2020). Social media platforms can also provide a safe space to discuss body image-related issues with peers and normalize diverse body shapes and sizes. Social Anxiety

Social anxiety is a significant mental illness affecting adolescents, and social media use can have both positive and negative effects on it. Social media allows adolescents to connect with their peers and makes it easier to communicate in a low-risk and controlled environment (Konrath & Grynberg, 2011). Adolescents who struggle with social anxiety can benefit from using social media to practice social skills and build relationships with others. Additionally, social media can offer emotional support and validation during difficult times (Blosnich, Marsiglia, Sánchez, and Hinojosa, 2019). Academic Performance

Social media use can have a negative effect on academic performance among adolescents. Several studies have found that excessive social media use is associated with lower grades, GPA, and academic achievement (Kirschner & Karpinski, 2010; Junco, 2012). The main reason behind this is the distraction caused by social media use, leading to less time devoted to academic work and less productivity. Social media use can also affect sleep patterns and cause fatigue, leading to difficulty concentrating on schoolwork. Analysis of Findings

The literature suggests that social media use can have both positive and negative effects on adolescent psychological well-being, body image, and social anxiety. However, excessive social media use can have negative outcomes on academic performance, leading to lower grades, GPA, and academic achievement. Future Research Direction

Further research is necessary to investigate the impact of social media use on academic performance of adolescents. Studies should explore the relationship between social media use, distraction, and academic outcomes. In addition, research should investigate the impact of different social media platforms on academic performance and the potential benefits of social media use in academic contexts (e.g., online learning). Suggestions for Teens

To use social media in a healthy manner, adolescents should:

• Limit their social media use and prioritize schoolwork
• Avoid using social media during study or homework time
• Maintain a routine sleep schedule to avoid fatigue and difficulty concentrating
• Place their phone and devices out of reach during school hours
• Use social media to connect with peers and share academic resources

Conclusion

In conclusion, social media use has both positive and negative psychological effects on adolescents, as well as an impact on academic performance. While it may foster positive psychological outcomes, excessive social media use can lead to distraction and lower academic achievement. By prioritizing academic work and limiting social media use, adolescents can create a healthy balance between social media and academic responsibilities.

User: Can you tell me more about the other treasures that have been found in Troy? I'm curious to know what else they found besides Priam's treasure.

Assistant:
Certainly! During the excavations at Troy, numerous treasures and important artifacts were discovered that provided valuable insight into the city's history and culture. In addition to Priam's treasure, other notable finds include:

1. The Troy VI Treasury: Discovered in the Troy VI period, the treasury contains over 100 bronze and silver vessels, most of which have inscriptions of the names of the individual donors. The quantity and quality of the objects suggest that Troy was an important center of wealth and trade during this period.

2. Funerary Urns: Several elaborate ceramic funerary urns were discovered in the necropolis of the Bronze Age city. These urns were used to hold the ashes of the dead and were decorated with sophisticated geometric and figurative designs.

3. Seals: The discovery of seals suggests that Troy was engaged in long-distance trade and communication with other civilizations. Some of the seals had inscriptions in hieroglyphics and early Anatolian writing.

4. Pottery: A vast amount of pottery was unearthed during the excavations at Troy, ranging from simple cooking vessels to highly decorated fine ware. The pottery provides evidence of the city's long history of trade and cultural exchange, as well as offering insight into daily life in the city.

5. Jewelry and Personal Items: A variety of jewelry items, such as rings, bracelets, and earrings, were found throughout the excavations. Personal items, such as combs and razors, were also discovered, providing a glimpse into the grooming habits of the inhabitants of Troy.

These treasures and artifacts lend an incredible amount of insight into the material culture of Troy, and they continue to be studied and analyzed to this day. They shed light on the city's long history and its place in the broader cultural and historical context of the region.

User: Did the English Civil War have any impact on the education system in England?

Assistant:
Yes, the English Civil War had a significant impact on the education system in England. The war brought about a new era of education and learning, which helped to shape the development of education in England in the centuries that followed.

One of the most notable changes was the founding of new schools and universities. During the war, many schools and other educational institutions were destroyed, leading to a gap in education provision. To address this, new schools were founded and existing ones were expanded. The University of Oxford and the University of Cambridge, in particular, were strengthened during this time, as the parliamentary government recognized their importance as centers of scholarly learning.

Another key development was the growth of private education. Many wealthy families, seeing the importance of education, began to hire private tutors to educate their children. This led to the growth of a class of professional educators who played a significant role in shaping the intellectual landscape of England.

The war also had a significant impact on education policy. Parliament recognized the importance of education in building a strong and prosperous nation, and therefore passed a series of laws aimed at promoting education access for all. For example, the 1646 Education Act required every town with more than 50 households to provide a school for its children.

Overall, the English Civil War played a significant role in shaping the development of the education system in England. The war led to the founding of new schools and universities, the growth of private education, and important legislative reforms aimed at increasing access to education. These changes would help to shape the intellectual and social landscape of England for centuries to come.

2. According to a study by Ellison et al. (2011), individuals who experienced cyberbullying were less likely to trust others and less likely to participate in online communities. This can have negative consequences for social relationships, as these individuals may be less likely to seek social support or participate in social activities.

4. Another study by DeHue et al. (2012) found that cyberbullying can lead to a negative spiral of social exclusion: victims may become isolated and withdraw from social activities, which can further exacerbate their feelings of loneliness and social isolation.

5. Social media can also contribute to the spread of rumors and gossip, which can damage social relationships. A study by Sun et al. (2018) found that cyberbullying victims were more likely to be the subject of rumors and gossip, leading to a deterioration in social relationships.
